self.declare_parameter('report_period',5.0); self.db={}; self.registry_pub=self.create_publisher(Target,'/targets/registry',20); self.unassigned_pub=self.create_publisher(Target,'/targets/unassigned',20); t.assigned_drone_id=old.assigned_drone_id; t.operator_notified=old.operator_notified\n        self.db[t.target_id]=t; self.publish(t)\n    def assigned_cb(self,t):\n        cur=self.db.get(t.target_id,t); cur.status=t.status; cur.assigned_drone_id=t.assigned_drone_id or cur.assigned_drone_id; cur.operator_notified=True; self.db[t.target_id]=cur; self.publish(cur)\n    def publish(self,t):\n        self.registry_pub.publish(t)\n        if t.status==Target.UNASSIGNED: self.unassigned_pub.publish(t)\n    def report(self):\n        counts={}\n        for t in self.db.values(): counts[t.status]=counts.get(t.status,0)+1\n        self.get_logger().info(f'Target registry: {len(self.db)} targets | {counts}')\ndef main(args=None):\n    rclpy.init(args=args); start < 65 and len(frames) < 15 and not errors:\n+    rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=.2)\n+result = {'result': 'FAIL', 'failure': ''}\n+try:\n+    assert not errors, f'cv_bridge: {errors[0]}'\n+    assert len(frames) >= 15, 'fewer than 15 actual image messages'\n+    assert camera_info, 'no CameraInfo message'\n+    assert all(f['width'] == 1280 and f['height'] == 960 for f in frames), 'wrong image resolution'\n+    assert all(f['stamp_ns'] > 0 and f['frame_id'] for f in frames), 'missing image stamp or frame ID'\n+    assert all(frames[i]['stamp_ns'] < frames[i + 1]['stamp_ns'] for i in range(len(frames) - 1)), 'image stamps not increasing'\n+    assert camera_info[-1].width == 1280 and camera_info[-1].height == 960, 'CameraInfo resolution mismatch'\n+    assert camera_info[-1].k[0] > 0 and camera_info[-1].k[4] > 0, 'CameraInfo focal lengths missing'\n+    result['result'] = 'PASS'\n+except AssertionError as exc:\n+    result['failure'] = str(exc)\n+finally:\n+    duration = frames[-1]['received_s'] - frames[0]['received_s'] if len(frames) > 1 else 0\n+    result.update(elapsed_s=round(time.monotonic() - start, 1),\n+                  frame_count=len(frames),\n+                  observed_fps=round((len(frames) - 1) / duration, 2) if duration > 0 else 0,\n+                  image=frames[-1] if frames else None,\n+                  camera_info={'width': camera_info[-1].width,\n+                               'height': camera_info[-1].height,\n+                               'frame_id': camera_info[-1].header.frame_id,\n+                               'fx': camera_info[-1].k[0],\n+                               'fy': camera_info[-1].k[4],\n+                               'cx': camera_info[-1].k[2],\n+                               'cy': camera_info[-1].k[5]} if camera_info else None,\n+                  conversion_errors=errors)\n+    with open(args.output, 'w') as stream:\n+        json.dump(result, stream, indent=2)\n+        stream.write('\\n')\n+    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)\n+    node.destroy_node()\n+    rclpy.shutdown()\n+raise SystemExit(0 if result['result'] == 'PASS' else 1)\n*** End Patch"));

self.declare_parameter('queue_depth',50); q=int(self.get_parameter('queue_depth').value); self.pub=self.create_publisher(Detection,'/detections/all',q); self.subs=[]\n        for i in range(1,9): self.subs.append(self.create_subscription(Detection,f'/drones/uav{i:02d}/detections',self.cb,q))\n    def cb(self,m): self.pub.publish(m)\ndef main(args=None):\n    rclpy.init(args=args); n=DetectionMux(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); rclpy.shutdown()\nimport threading, datetime\nimport rclpy\nfrom rclpy.node import Node\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.msg import Target, OperatorAlert\nfrom wildlife_interfaces.srv import OperatorDecision\nclass OperatorInterface(Node):\n    def __init__(self):\n        super().__init__('operator_interface'); self.create_subscription(OperatorAlert,'/operator/alerts',self.alert_cb,20)\n        if bool(self.get_parameter('input_enabled').value): threading.Thread(target=self.keyboard_loop,daemon=True).start()\n        self.get_logger().info('Operator interface ready: r <drone_id> = RETURN; h <drone_id> = CONTINUE HOLD')\n    def alert_cb(self,a):\n        if a.severity!=OperatorAlert.ACTION_REQUIRED: return\n        self.pending[a.drone_id]=a\n        print(\n            f\"\\n[OPERATOR ACTION] drone={a.drone_id} target={a.target_id} \"\n            f\"class={a.target_class} confidence={a.confidence:.0%} \"\n            f\"position=({a.target_position.x:.1f},{a.target_position.y:.1f})\\n\"\n            f\"  r {a.drone_id} -> RETURN\\n\"\n            f\"  h {a.drone_id} -> CONTINUE HOLD\\n\"\n        )\n    def keyboard_loop(self):\n        while rclpy.ok():\n            try: parts=input().strip().split()\n            except EOFError: return\n            if len(parts)==2 and parts[0].lower() in ('r','h'): self.apply(parts[1],OperatorDecision.Request.RETURN if parts[0].lower()=='r' else OperatorDecision.Request.CONTINUE_HOLD)\n    def service_cb(self,req,res):\n        ok=self.apply(req.assigned_drone_id,req.decision,req.target_id); res.accepted=ok; res.message='Decision applied' if ok else 'No pending alert for drone/target'; res.timestamp=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(); return res\n    def apply(self,drone_id,decision,target_id=''):\n        if decision not in (OperatorDecision.Request.RETURN, OperatorDecision.Request.CONTINUE_HOLD):\n            return False\n        a=self.pending.get(drone_id)\n        if a is None or (target_id and a.target_id!=target_id): self.get_logger().warning(f'No pending alert for {drone_id} target={target_id}'); return False\n        t=Target(); t.header.stamp=self.get_clock().now().to_msg(); t.target_id=a.target_id; t.assigned_drone_id=drone_id; t.operator_notified=True; t.status=Target.CLEARED if decision==OperatorDecision.Request.RETURN else Target.STANDOFF_HOLD; self.pub.publish(t); self.pending.pop(drone_id,None) if decision == OperatorDecision.Request.RETURN else None; self.get_logger().info(f'Operator decision {drone_id}: {\"RETURN\" if t.status==Target.CLEARED else \"CONTINUE HOLD\"}'); return True\ndef main(args=None):\n    rclpy.init(args=args); n=OperatorInterface(); rclpy.spin(n); n.destroy_node(); rclpy.shutdown()\n"}

[310] tool exec call: text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Delete File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/ARCHITECTURE.md\n*** Add File: /home/mohammed-ali/Downloads/gpt /wildlife_ws_ros2_jazzy_v0_3/ARCHITECTURE.md\n+# Wildlife ROS 2 architecture — V0.4 development\n+\n+This continues the confirmed V0.3 nine-package workspace. Typed messages,\n+services and actions remain unchanged. PX4 owns flight stabilization and native\n+failsafes; ROS owns mission intent, observation, assignment, standoff requests\n+and operator decisions. There is one PX4 adapter per real vehicle and no\n+second mission FSM.\n+\n+```text\n+wildlife_interfaces   frozen Detection, Target, DroneState and control contracts\n+wildlife_core         shared math and RViz markers\n+wildlife_vehicles     UGV logic, readiness-aware deployment, logical UAV, docking\n+wildlife_perception   deterministic detector_sim; legacy logical OpenCV;\n+                      real PX4-camera OpenCV and ray/ground geometry\n+wildlife_targets      detection mux, tracking/fusion, persistent target registry\n+wildlife_swarm        target-to-available-UAV assignment\n+wildlife_mission      mission FSM and typed operator service\n+wildlife_simulation   Gazebo worlds, UGV adapter, existing PX4 UAV adapter\n+wildlife_bringup      launch profiles, parameters and RViz configuration\n+```\n+\n+## Observation and mission flow\n+\n+```text\n+Gazebo camera -> ros_gz_bridge -> sensor_msgs/Image + CameraInfo\n+PX4 attitude --------------------\\\n+PX4 local position -> PX4 adapter -> DroneState(map pose)\n+                                   \\\n+                                    OpenCV + camera geometry -> Detection(map)\n+detector_sim -------------------------------------------> Detection(map)\n+Detection -> detection_mux -> tracker -> Target manager -> coordinator\n+          -> assigned Target -> existing UAV behavior -> PX4 adapter\n+          -> namespaced PX4 command/setpoint -> PX4 -><truncated omitted_approx_tokens="69" />ently validated for uav01 only. The older V0.2\n+OpenCV node remains available for its logical camera demo; it does not provide\n+the V0.4 map projection. YOLO is optional and has not been added.\n+\n+## PX4 fleet identity\n+\n+Each PX4 process uses instance `i-1`, system ID and XRCE-DDS key `i`, DDS\n+namespace `/uavNN`, and one correspondingly named ROS adapter. For the tested\n+two-flight profile, uav01 spawns at (0, 0) m and uav02 at (0, -35) m. The\n+adapter adds this origin to local NED-to-ENU position and subtracts it for\n+Offboard setpoints. PX4 command targets use the matching system ID; ACKs are\n+accepted only from the adapter's external source on that vehicle's namespaced\n+topic. Gazebo entities are `x500_0` and `x500_1`. Eight-vehicle structural\n+testing uses 12 m spaced columns in two rows; eight-vehicle flight has not\n+been validated.\n+\n+The real camera profile mounts the pinned `x500_mono_cam_down` on uav01 and\n+bridges its observed Gazebo image topic to\n+`/drones/uav01/camera/image_raw`. Pixel rays use CameraInfo, PX4 FRD/NED\n+attitude and measured map position to intersect a flat ground plane. Detailed\n+frame definitions and timing limits are in `docs/V0_4_CAMERA_GEOMETRY.md`.\n+\n+## Deployment and completion\n+\n+The UGV requests SINGLE or SPLIT deployment. The deployment node filters\n+active IDs, waits for recent per-vehicle readiness, and sends typed LAUNCH.\n+The PX4 adapter queues LAUNCH until ready; flight mode, arming, position and\n+landing are measured from PX4.
